The award, or... the supervisor thing. It just feels like a mistake, I guess. Like I tricked them into thinking I'm better than I am.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any days in the past two months where you felt somewhat better, or has it been consistently this way?\n\nPatient: I don't think so. It's just... been like this. For about two months, I guess. Ever since that... that window display award. And the district manager coming around. I just don't feel anything when I do those things. I just kind of stand there.
